# Showcase coverage dispositions for the bounded source inventory.
#
# Each current inventory ID has either a tested example in declarations_data.rs
# or one explicit disposition here. This is not a claim that every ID is behaviorally tested.
#
#   id | reason | SCn       existing roadmap step; still pending behavior review
#   id | reason | review    explicit review required; not an approved exclusion
#
# Legacy permanent decisions are retained as review rows until independently verified.
# Page/title associations are leads only and never replace a proof or disposition.
# A stale ID or an ID with both a proof and disposition fails the gate.
